Keep ROS/Gazebo discovery on loopback\\n# so host Wi-Fi/interface changes cannot interrupt the simulated vehicle graph.\\nexport ROS_LOCALHOST_ONLY=\"${ROS_LOCALHOST_ONLY:-1}\"\\nexport GZ_IP=\"${GZ_IP:-127.0.0.1}\"\\n'''\np.write_text(s)\np=Path('src/wildlife_simulation/wildlife_simulation/px4_uav_adapter.py')\ns=p.read_text().replace(\"            if self.stream_start is not None:\\n\", \"            if self.stream_start is not None and self.state not in (DroneState.STORED, DroneState.DOCKED):\\n\")\ns=s.replace(\"        if self.fault and self.state not in (DroneState.RETURNING, DroneState.DOCKED):\", '''        # A completed landing is terminal even if later transport or preflight\n        # health changes. For a faulted return, require fresh measured landing,\n        # disarm and proximity to the latched home before logical docking.\n        if (self.state == DroneState.PAUSED and self.was_airborne and self.land.landed\n                and self.status.arming_state == VehicleStatus.ARMING_STATE_DISARMED\n                and math.hypot(self.x - self.home_x, self.y - self.home_y) <= 2.0):\n            self.state = DroneState.DOCKED\n        if self.state == DroneState.DOCKED:\n            self.publish()\n            return\n        if self.fault and self.state not in (DroneState.RETURNING, DroneState.DOCKED):''')\np.write_text(s)\np=Path('tests/test_px4_adapter.py')\ns=p.read_text().replace(\"if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()\", '''    def test_confirmed_docking_survives_later_telemetry_loss(self):\n        n=self.n; n.state=DroneState.DOCKED; n.was_airborne=True\n        n.stream_start=time.monotonic()-2; n.received.clear()\n        n.tick()\n        self.assertEqual(n.state,DroneState.DOCKED)\n        self.assertIsNone(n.fault)\n\n    def test_faulted_native_landing_needs_fresh_home_landing_and_disarm(self):\n        n=self.n; n.was_airborne=True; n.fail('native recovery')\n        n.home_x,n.home_y=n.x,n.y\n        n.land_cb(VehicleLandDetected(landed=False))\n        n.tick(); self.assertEqual(n.state,DroneState.PAUSED)\n        n.land_cb(VehicleLandDetected(landed=True))\n        n.status_cb(VehicleStatus(arming_state=VehicleStatus.ARMING_STATE_ARMED))\n        n.tick(); self.assertEqual(n.state,DroneState.PAUSED)\n        n.status_cb(VehicleStatus(arming_state=VehicleStatus.ARMING_STATE_DISARMED))\n        n.home_x+=10.\n        n.tick(); self.assertEqual(n.state,DroneState.PAUSED)\n        n.home_x=n.x\n        n.tick(); self.assertEqual(n.state,DroneState.DOCKED)\n        self.assertFalse(n.heartbeat_pub.messages)\n\nif __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()''')\np.write_text(s)\nPY","max_output_tokens":1700}));

text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"python3 - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\np=Path('src/wildlife_bringup/launch/single_deployment.launch.py')\ns=p.read_text().replace('    nodes=[\\n', \"    nodes=[\\n      Node(package='tf2_ros',executable='static_transform_publisher',name='wildlife_map_frame',arguments=['--frame-id','wildlife_world','--child-frame-id','map'],output='screen'),\\n\")\np.write_text(s)\nPY\ncat > docs/V0_4_SCOPE.md <<'EOF'\n# V0.4 scope and release gates\n\nV0.4 continues `v0.3-final` in the same nine-package ROS 2 Jazzy workspace.\nThe typed interfaces and existing PX4 adapter/mission FSM remain authoritative.\nThis is academic, non-contact observation: PX4 stabilizes the vehicles and\nowns native failsafes; ROS manages deployment, search, targets, standoff and\nhuman operator decisions. No payload, contact or pursuit behavior is added.\n\nImplemented scope includes independent PX4 identities/commands/telemetry,\nreal Gazebo down-cameras, OpenCV pixel-to-map ground-plane localization,\npersistent tracking and multi-source fusion, descriptive target velocity,\nhealth exclusion and confirmed-return reassignment, lower-load camera\nprofiles, separate fleet search cells, and examiner visualization.\n\nThe eight-aircraft demonstration uses selected lightweight backends: no\ncameras or YOLO on the fleet. Camera missions use one or two 640×480/10 Hz\ncameras. YOLO is optional and unimplemented. The logical UGV continues <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="807" />arm/COMPLETE. Its YAML profile keeps local position's one-second\nreceipt-age limit; slower status/landing streams allow three seconds because\nPX4 LandDetected publishes at one Hz of simulation time. It remains a basic\nmission-planning demonstration, not general collision avoidance.\n\n## Examiner demonstration with real images\n\n```bash\nHEADLESS= SHOW_RVIZ=true OPERATOR_HOLD_S=15 \\\n  CAMERA_EVIDENCE_FILE=docs/validation/px4_examiner_demo.json \\\n  scripts/validate_px4_real_camera_mission.sh\n```\n\nThis uses the automated simulated operator for reproducible HOLD then RETURN.\nWatch the logical UGV and its Gazebo display proxy, readiness-aware launch,\nactual camera observations, target assignment, standoff, RViz labels and\nassignment line, operator hold, PX4 RTL, landing/disarm and COMPLETE.\nQGC shows native vehicle identity, flight mode, arming, position and battery.\nROS remains the mission authority.\n\nFor a human-operated session, start `scripts/start_px4_camera.sh detector_backend:=opencv show_rviz:=true`\nand inspect `/operator/alerts`. Call the existing `/operator/decision` service\nwith the observed target and UAV IDs. Decision 1 is CONTINUE_HOLD; 0 is RETURN.\nFor example, only after the alert identifies `tgt_001` and `uav01`:\n\n```bash\nros2 service call /operator/decision wildlife_interfaces/srv/OperatorDecision \\\n  '{target_id: tgt_001, assigned_drone_id: uav01, decision: 1}'\n```\n\nCamera scripts use ROS domain 86 by default. Source the environment and set\n`ROS_DOMAIN_ID=86` in the operator terminal. Use RETURN later to finish.\nDOCKED does not claim physical tray contact. The Gazebo UGV proxy has no\ncollision geometry and does not feed physical arrival into deployment.\n\nThe separate logic-only demo remains `subject1_demo.launch.py` and does not\nrequire PX4 or camera processing. The original logical Gazebo camera profile\nis also preserved. No YOLO model or dependency is needed.\nEOF","max_output_tokens":1200}));
